### [1] [JWST Observations of RR Lyrae in Leo P](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20692)
*Catherine M. Slaughter,Alessandro Savino,Meredith Durbin,Andrew E. Dolphin,Kristen B. W. McQuinn,Max J. B. Newman,Evan D. Skillman,Daniel R. Weisz*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用HST与JWST对Leo P中天琴座RR型变星进行联合测光分析，首次建立基于JWST的PWZ关系并验证了测距的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低金属丰度环境下天琴座RR型变星的研究对理解贫金属星族及宇宙距离尺度具有重要意义，而JWST提供了新的观测窗口，需要评估其在该领域的潜力并建立可靠的标定关系。

Method: 分析Leo P中同空间覆盖的HST/ACS与JWST/NIRCam时间序列测光数据，识别RR Lyrae样本（HST 20颗、JWST 15颗），测定周期、振幅与平均星等，拟合PWZ函数，并联合WLM的既有JWST观测建立JWST波段的PWZ关系。

Result: 基于HST样本得到与Gaia一致的Leo P距离为μ=26.04±0.07（1.61±0.06 Mpc）；联合拟合使斜率平均分数不确定度降低31%、截距降低11%。

Conclusion: JWST可用于贫金属RR Lyrae研究并有效提升PWZ关系的约束能力，为未来利用JWST开展该类测距工作奠定基础。

Abstract: We analyze co-spatial HST/ACS and JWST/NIRCam time-series photometric observations of RR Lyrae in the low-metallicity dwarf galaxy Leo P. This study provides a new opportunity to explore the properties of and potential for metal-poor RR Lyrae studies with JWST using well-understood relationships from past HST work. We identify 20 RR Lyrae in the HST imaging and 15 RR Lyrae in the JWST imaging. We determine the periods, amplitudes, and mean magnitudes for each of the RR Lyrae. We fit the Period-Wesenheit-Metallicity (PWZ) function to the HST RR Lyrae population, and obtain a Gaia-consistent RR Lyrae-based distance to Leo P of $μ= 26.04\pm0.07$ ($1.61\pm0.06$ Mpc), consistent with existing distance measurements. We determine a JWST-based PWZ using Leo P data along with previous JWST RR Lyrae observations in WLM. The simultaneous fit decreases the mean fractional uncertainty by 31% on the slope and by 11% on the intercept, increasing the constraining power as compared to the existing calibration.

</details>


### [2] [The Impact of High-Mass Clusters on the Binary Black Hole Merger Rate over Cosmic Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20698)
*Aidan Mai,Kyle Kremer,Fulya Kıroğlu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过Cluster Monte Carlo模型网格计算了致密星团产生的双黑洞并合率，发现本地并合率由低质量、高金属丰度星团主导，而层级并合率对星团初始质量函数的高质量端（Schechter质量）高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密星团是双黑洞并合的重要形成通道，但此前缺乏覆盖完整观测参数空间（星团质量、金属丰度、维里半径）并包含超大质量星团模型的全宇宙学并合率计算，且层级并合贡献对星团初始质量函数高质量端的依赖尚不明确。

Method: 采用Cluster Monte Carlo (CMC) 模型网格，覆盖观测到的星团质量、金属丰度和维里半径参数空间，首次纳入N=$10^7$粒子模型（含新的太阳金属丰度模型），扩展CMC星团目录至本地宇宙最重星团；使用解析星团形成模型，将星团形成率与模型套件的延迟时间分布卷积，计算宇宙学BBH并合率。

Result: 本地星团BBH并合率由来自新形成、高金属丰度星团的低质量并合（$M_1 \lesssim 20\,M_\odot$）主导；高质量星团是层级并合的主要场所，但其贡献被星团初始质量函数的指数截断抑制。层级并合率对Schechter质量$M_S$高度敏感。模型无需精细调节即可复现观测到的当前星系团数密度，$M_S=10^7\,M_\odot$与Virgo星系团质量分布最吻合，此时27%的本地动力学并合为层级并合（对比规范值$M_S=10^{6.26}\,M_\odot$时的18%）。

Conclusion: 引力波对层级并合比例的约束可独立限制星团初始质量函数的高质量端；公开提供的并合权重可用于与LVK分布及其他形成模型的直接比较。

Abstract: We present a cosmological calculation of the binary black hole merger rate from dense star clusters, using a $\texttt{Cluster Monte Carlo}$ ($\texttt{CMC}$) model grid spanning the full observed parameter space of cluster mass, metallicity, and virial radius. For the first time, we incorporate $N=10^{7}$-particle $\texttt{CMC}$ models into this calculation, including a new solar-metallicity model, extending the $\texttt{CMC Cluster Catalog}$ to the most massive clusters observed in the local Universe. Using an analytic cluster formation model which convolves the cluster formation rate with delay-time distributions from our model suite, we find that the local cluster BBH merger rate is dominated by low-mass ($M_1 \lesssim 20\,M_\odot$) mergers originating from recently formed, high-metallicity clusters. While high-mass clusters are the primary sites of hierarchical (second-generation and higher) mergers, their contribution to the overall merger rate is suppressed by the exponential cutoff of the cluster initial mass function. The hierarchical merger rate is therefore highly sensitive to the exact value of the Schechter mass, $M_S$, suggesting that gravitational wave constraints on the hierarchical merger fraction can independently constrain the high-mass end of the cluster initial mass function. Our cluster formation model reproduces the observed present-day GC number density without fine tuning, and we find $M_S = 10^7\,M_\odot$ provides the best match to the observed Virgo GC mass distribution. For this value, $27\%$ of local dynamical mergers are hierarchical, compared to $18\%$ for the canonical value of $M_S = 10^{6.26}\,M_\odot$. Our merger weights, which we make publicly available, can be used for direct comparison with LVK distributions and other formation models.

</details>


### [3] [CoRGI in MaNGA - II. NGC 5717: cold-gas accretion and disk growth in a BGG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20704)
*Damir Gasymov,Ivan Yu. Katkov,Eva K. Grebel,Anna Pasquali,Evgenii V. Rubtsov,Katarina Kraljic,Igor V. Chilingarian,Kirill A. Grishin,Anna S. Saburova*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究报告了首例在亮群星系中发现的大质量反向旋转恒星盘——NGC 5717，其冷反向旋转盘嵌于古老的动力学热椭球成分中，年轻、贫金属且延伸，支持由外部吸积气体原位形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模恒星反向旋转通常被解释为外部气体吸积的标志，此类系统多存在于较稀疏的环境中。但在大质量中心星系中，其群或星系团环境可能加热、剥离或扰动冷气体，该通道能否运作尚不清楚。本文旨在探究大质量中心星系中是否也能发生此类过程。

Method: 结合SDSS-IV MaNGA积分视场光谱、CFHT成像、辅助HI数据，以及周边群内星系的SDSS/DESI光谱，并采用化学演化模型进行分析。

Result: NGC 5717包含一个动力学冷的反向旋转盘，嵌于古老的动力学热椭球成分中。该盘年轻（约1.3 Gyr）、贫金属（[Fe/H]约-0.7 dex）、延伸（h约2.4 kpc）、大质量（log M*/M☉约9.9）；其电离气体与盘共转，而现有HI数据显示无显著剩余中性原子气体储库（log M_HI/M☉≲9）。

Conclusion: 这些性质支持次级成分由外部吸积气体原位形成。化学演化模型倾向于来自宇宙纤维状结构的气体吸积，与周围宇宙网几何一致，并指示高效的爆发式恒星形成及大量金属增丰外流。NGC 5717可能是大质量中心星系中罕见的盘生长案例，由群组装历史早期阶段的外部冷气体吸积所促成。

Abstract: Large-scale stellar counter-rotation, in which two coplanar stellar components rotate in opposite directions, is usually interpreted as a signature of external gas accretion. Such systems are most often found in relatively sparse environments, where cold gas can be acquired and remain dynamically undisturbed long enough to settle into a disk. It is less clear whether this channel can operate in massive central galaxies, whose group or cluster environments can heat, strip, or perturb cold gas. Here we present NGC 5717, one of the most massive galaxies known to host a counter-rotating stellar disk and the first identified case in a brightest group galaxy. Using integral-field spectroscopy from SDSS-IV Mapping Nearby Galaxies at Apache Point Observatory (MaNGA), together with CFHT imaging, ancillary HI data, and SDSS/DESI spectroscopy of the surrounding group, we show that the galaxy contains a dynamically cold counter-rotating disk embedded in an old dynamically hot elliptical component. The disk is young ($\sim 1.3$ Gyr), metal-poor ([Fe/H] $\sim -0.7$ dex), extended ($h \sim 2.4$ kpc), and massive ($\log M_\star/M_\odot \sim 9.9$); the associated ionized gas co-rotates with it, while the available HI data indicate no significant remaining reservoir of neutral atomic gas ($\log M_{\mathrm{HI}}/M_\odot \lesssim 9$). These properties support an in situ origin of the secondary component from externally accreted gas. The chemical evolution model favors gas accretion from a cosmic filament, which is consistent with the surrounding cosmic-web geometry, and indicates highly efficient burst-like star formation with substantial metal-enriched outflows. NGC 5717 may therefore represent a rare example of disk growth in a massive central galaxy, enabled by external cold-gas accretion during an earlier phase of the group assembly history.

</details>


### [4] [How Little Red Dots Turn Blue: Evolution of Kelvin-Helmholtz Contracting Black Hole Envelopes and Observational Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20709)
*Zihao Wang,Kohei Inayoshi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模型，用快速增长的、被厚气体包层包裹的黑洞加核区恒星形成，统一解释JWST观测到的小红点（LRDs）和小蓝点（LBDs），并通过包层的开尔文-亥姆霍兹收缩把它们联系为同一现象的演化两阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST发现紧凑、偏红的宽线发射体（LRDs）以及偏蓝的对应群体（LBDs），但二者物理联系不明：可能是同一现象的不同演化阶段、不同群体，或早期星系及其黑洞演化中的不同阶段。作者希望给出一个能同时解释两者并连接二者的物理模型。

Method: 构建模型：一个快速增长的黑洞嵌在几何厚的气体包层中，并伴随核区恒星形成。模型拟合LRDs与LBDs的观测SED，并通过气体供给因外部恒星活动或反馈而下降时包层的开尔文-亥姆霍兹收缩及核区吸积盘暴露，自然连接两个阶段。

Result: 模型成功重现LRDs与LBDs的SED，并在UV-光学斜率（β_UV-β_opt）平面上预测演化轨迹：轨迹从LRD区域出发，出现向(β_UV, β_opt)~(2,-1)的红端偏移，再转向LBD区域。该特征对控制包层结构的模型参数变化基本不敏感，是过渡群体的稳健标志。过渡持续约5-50 Myr，主要取决于黑洞反馈效率与包层的气体保留效率。

Conclusion: 过渡系统与LRDs的丰度比可约束早期超大质量黑洞快速增长期间的黑洞反馈和气体保留过程。

Abstract: James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) observations have revealed compact, red-colored, broad-line emitters known as little red dots (LRDs), while a bluer counterpart population has been referred to as little blue dots (LBDs). Their physical connection remains unclear: they may represent different evolutionary stages of the same phenomenon, distinct populations, or different phases of the broader evolution of early galaxies and their black holes (BHs). We propose a model in which a rapidly growing BH is embedded in a geometrically thick gaseous envelope accompanied by concurrent nuclear star formation. The model reproduces the observed spectral energy distributions (SEDs) of LRDs and LBDs, and naturally connects the two phases through Kelvin-Helmholtz contraction of the envelope and exposure of the nuclear accretion disk, as the gas supply declines due to external stellar activity or feedback. We predict the corresponding evolutionary tracks in the UV-optical slope ($β_{\rm UV}$-$β_{\rm opt}$) plane which bridge the apparent gap between LRDs and LBDs. These evolutionary tracks start from the LRD regime and consistently exhibit a red excursion that extends toward $(β_{\rm UV},β_{\rm opt})\sim(2,-1)$ before turning toward the LBD regime. This feature is largely insensitive to variations in the model parameters that govern the envelope structure, providing a robust signature of the transitional population. The transition lasts $\sim5$-$50\,\mathrm{Myr}$, depending primarily on the BH feedback efficiency and the gas-retention efficiency of the envelope. The abundance ratio of transitional systems to LRDs therefore provides a constraint on BH feedback and gas retention during the rapid growth of early supermassive BHs.

</details>


### [5] [The Pristine survey XXIX. Data Release 2: Photometric metallicities for 15 millions stars down to $G\sim 20.5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20714)
*Zhen Yuan,Anke Ardern-Arentsen,Nicolas F. Martin,Else Starkenburg,Morgan Fouesneau,Piercarlo Bonifacio,Elisabetta Caffau,Vanessa Hill,Georges Kordopatis,Camila Navarrete,Julio Navarro,Samuel Rusterucci,Federico Sestito,Salvatore Taibi,Kim A. Venn,Akshara Viswanathan,HanYuan Zhang,Giuseppina Battaglia*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: Pristine巡天发布DR2，覆盖约8000 deg²，为1500万FGK星提供测光金属丰度，并给出20万+极贫金属星和6500+超贫金属星候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窄带测光巡天是测量银河系恒星金属丰度的有效手段。Pristine DR1虽提供了高精度绝对定标，但深度受限于Gaia分光光度（G≲17.65），覆盖约6500 deg²。为利用巡天全部深度探测暗弱的贫金属晕，需扩展覆盖并深入更暗星等。

Method: 处理2015年3月至2025年1月的全部Pristine观测图像，沿用DR1方法。定标核心是利用具有Gaia合成CaHK_syn星等的亮星子集训练PhotCalib神经网络，随后使用已建立的Pristine (CaHK, G, G_BP, G_RP) 到 [Fe/H] 模型推导测光金属丰度。

Result: DR2相比DR1新增约1500 deg²覆盖，并包含暗至G~20.5的暗源。最终星表覆盖约8000 deg²，为1500万FGK星提供测光金属丰度，与独立的DESI金属丰度吻合良好。报告超过20万个极贫金属星候选体和6500个超贫金属星候选体。

Conclusion: DR2通过扩大覆盖范围和深入更暗星等显著增加了巡天的体积，可用于揭示暗弱的贫金属晕，并为大规模贫金属星研究提供重要数据基础。

Abstract: Tailored narrow-band photometric observations are an efficient way to measure the metallicity of Milky Way stars. The Pristine survey has been mapping the northern sky since 2015 using narrow-band $CaHK$ photometry obtained with the MegaCam imager on the Canada-France-Hawaii Telescope (CFHT). Data Release 1 (DR1) provided a high-precision absolute calibration of the survey observed before October 2022 using \Gaia DR3 XP synthetic photometry, yielding photometric metallicities over $\sim$6,500 deg$^2$, but was limited in depth to the availability of \Gaia spectro-photometry ($G \lesssim 17.65$). We present the Pristine Data Release 2 (DR2) that includes all Pristine observations until January 2025, corresponding to an additional 1,500\,deg$^2$ of coverage, and including all faint sources down to $G\sim20.5$. This release aims to exploit the full depth of the Pristine observations to uncover the faint metal-poor halo. We process all images observed from the start of the survey in March 2015 through to January 2025 following the methodology presented in DR1. The core of the calibration utilizes the subset of bright stars with \Gaia synthetic $CaHK_\mathrm{syn}$ magnitudes to train the \texttt{PhotCalib} neural network. We then derive photometric metallicities using the established Pristine ($CaHK, G, G_{\rm BP}, G_{\rm RP}$) to [Fe/H] model. The resulting catalogue covers approximately $\sim$ 8,000 deg$^{2}$ and provides photometric metallicities for 15 millions FGK stars, which show good agreement with recently published, independent Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) metallicities. With its increased coverage and access to a significantly fainter regime compared to DR1, DR2 significantly increases the volume of the survey. We report more than 200,000 and 6,500 very and extremely metal-poor star candidates, respectively.

</details>


### [6] [HI rotation curves and mass-size relation for 568 galaxies from the Apertif Wide-area Extragalactic Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20718)
*Konstantin Haubner,Federico Lelli,Enrico Di Teodoro,Kelley M. Hess,Francis Duey,Stacy McGaugh,James Schombert,Elizabeth A. K. Adams,W. J. G. de Blok,Danielle M. Lucero,Helga Dénes,Julián Garrido,Joeri van Leeuwen,Manuel Parra-Royón,Anastasia A. Ponomareva,Susana Sánchez Exposito*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文发布Apertif广域河外星系巡天（AWES）的首批HI运动学模型数据，建立了迄今最大的均匀HI旋转曲线样本（568个可靠模型），并基于表面密度轮廓研究HI质量-尺度关系，发现该关系极为紧密且HI盘平均表面密度近似恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展的HI旋转曲线是研究星系演化、检验暗物质与修改引力模型的有力工具，但受射电干涉仪巡天速度限制，以往样本规模仅数百个星系。作者希望借助AWES巡天大幅扩充均匀的HI运动学样本。

Method: 从AWES探测到的1924个HI星系出发，构建568个可靠运动学模型，得到旋转曲线、速度色散轮廓、表面密度轮廓及HI盘几何；并利用运动学建模导出的HI表面密度轮廓，以213个均匀样本研究HI质量-尺度关系。

Result: 该数据集为迄今最大均匀HI旋转曲线样本：HI盘质量跨三个数量级，外旋转速度约20–300 km/s，延伸2至60+ kpc，旋转曲线数据点3–23个（峰值4）。旋转曲线内区形态多样，大半径处趋于平坦。HI质量-尺度关系很紧密，质量方向观测弥散约0.101 dex，内禀弥散仅0.067±0.007 dex，最佳拟合斜率1.96±0.03（以质量对尺度拟合），表明HI盘平均表面密度近乎恒定。

Conclusion: AWES首批数据发布提供了迄今最大的均匀HI旋转曲线样本，为星系演化及暗物质/修改引力研究提供重要资源；HI质量-尺度关系的紧致性和近恒定表面密度进一步支持HI盘结构的规律性。

Abstract: The extended HI rotation curves of galaxies are one of the most powerful tools for studying galaxy evolution and for testing different dark matter and modified gravity models. However, their sample sizes have historically been limited to a few hundred galaxies, mostly due to the survey speed of radio interferometers. Here, we present a first data release of HI kinematic models from the Apertif Wide-area Extragalactic Survey (AWES). Starting from 1924 galaxies detected in HI, we constructed 568 reliable kinematic models, which provide rotation curves, velocity dispersion profiles, surface density profiles, and HI disk geometries. This dataset constitutes the largest homogeneous HI rotation curve sample to date. The HI disks of the modeled galaxies span three orders of magnitude in HI mass, have outer rotation velocities between ~20 and ~300 km/s, and extend from 2 to more than 60 kpc. The number of data points of the rotation curves ranges from 3 to 23, with a peak value at 4 and a broad tail to larger values. The rotation curves display a variety of inner shapes, but in general, they tend to become flat at large radii. Based on the HI surface density profiles derived during the kinematic modeling, we studied the HI mass-size relation with a large homogeneous sample of 213 galaxies. The relation is very tight, with an observed scatter along the mass direction of ~0.101 dex and an inferred intrinsic scatter along the same direction of only 0.067 +/- 0.007 dex. The best fitting slope is 1.96 +/- 0.03 when fitting for mass as a function of size, indicating that HI disks have a constant average surface density.

</details>


### [7] [Wandering intermediate-mass black holes and off-nuclear tidal disruption events from early star clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20719)
*Claire E. Williams,Smadar Naoz,Yihao Zhou,Tatsuya Akiba-Lewis,Mark Vogelsberger,Blakesley Burkhart,Naoki Yoshida,Avi Chen,Kyle Kremer,William Lake,Federico Marinacci,Shyam H. Menon*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过半解析模型研究高红移致密星团中经失控恒星碰撞形成的漫游中等质量黑洞（IMBH），预测其在宇宙演化中产生的离核潮汐瓦解事件（TDE）特征与发生率，为约束高红移IMBH星团族群提供框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离核TDE揭示了银河系中心之外存在大质量黑洞，但这一"漫游"黑洞族群的起源尚不明确，需要理论模型来预测其可观测信号。

Method: 构建半解析模型：在7<z<20的低质量子晕中采样星团，采用IllustrisTNG TNG50的并合树追踪15个恒星质量10^9-10^11 M_sun椭圆星系的组装；对比两种高红移星团形成模型（基于高分辨率模拟的"极大"模型与基于本地星团关系标定的"保守"模型）。

Result: 离核TDE在IMBH形成后10-100 Myr内成爆发式出现（因黑洞周围损耗锥被耗尽）；事件通常发生在距星系中心10-10^3 kpc处，发生率随星系质量增加；在极大模型中，最大质量星系的前身星系在7≲z≲13达到约5×10^-4至4×10^-2 yr^-1 的峰值离核TDE发生率。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个框架，可通过离核TDE在高红移宇宙中的贡献率来约束那些经失控坍缩形成IMBH的早期星团族群。

Abstract: Off-nuclear tidal disruption events (TDEs) reveal evidence for massive black holes outside galactic centers, but the origins of this wandering population remain uncertain. We investigate wandering intermediate-mass black holes (IMBHs) formed through runaway stellar collisions in dense, high-redshift star clusters and predict their TDE signatures across cosmic time. Our semi-analytic model samples clusters associated with low-mass subhalos at $7<z<20$ and uses merger trees from the IllustrisTNG TNG50 simulation to follow the assembly of 15 elliptical galaxies with stellar masses of $10^9-10^{11}~M_\odot$. Abstract: We report the identification of a UV attenuation bump in the Black Pearl, a galaxy at a spectroscopic redshift of 9.3335 observed with JWST/NIRSpec as part of the ZENITH and EMBER surveys. This is the highest-redshift UV bump reported to date, probing dust properties only approximately 500 Myr after the Big Bang. The feature is directly detected as a broad depression in the rest-frame UV continuum and is well described by a local Drude profile, with a central wavelength of 2132+/-17 Angstroms, an FWHM of 413+/-54 Angstroms, and a peak attenuation of 0.21+/-0.02 mag. Joint spectrophotometric modeling with a flexible attenuation law independently recovers a pronounced UV bump without imposing its presence a priori, regardless of the adopted star-formation history. The modeling indicates a relatively low-mass, actively star-forming galaxy, with a logarithmic stellar mass of 8.35 (+0.09/-0.08) in solar units, a star-formation rate of 2.36 (+0.54/-0.42) solar masses per year, and a visual attenuation of 0.34 (+0.12/-0.11) mag. Rest-frame optical emission-line diagnostics indicate a hard ionizing spectrum and allow for a moderate AGN contribution, but provide no evidence for a powerful AGN capable of dominating the observed UV continuum. The bump is blueshifted by 43+/-17 Angstroms (approximately 2.5 sigma) relative to the canonical Milky Way 2175 Angstrom feature and by 127 +/- 17 Angstroms relative to the few UV bumps currently known at redshift approximately 7, while its width is consistent with the broad distribution measured along Milky Way sightlines. The displaced central wavelength therefore provides the clearest evidence for a nonstandard UV-bump profile at redshift 9.3335, pointing to substantial diversity in the properties of UV-bump carriers at early cosmic times, with both PAHs and small graphite grains remaining plausible candidates.

Conclusion: 作者提出一个近乎正面对撞的情景：Cen 45近期穿过Cen 30，两个子团大致沿视线方向运动。该情景可解释子团间较大的视向速度差、二者之间的热X射线气体、球状星团桥以及NGC 4709膨胀的球状星团系统。

Abstract: Intracluster globular clusters (GCs) preserve the hierarchical and chemical history of galaxy cluster assembly. The nearby Centaurus cluster is believed to be undergoing an interaction between two subclusters, Cen 30 and Cen 45, which should leave an imprint on its intracluster GCs. We aim to characterise the GC population in Centaurus and constrain its recent assembly history, ultimately constructing an interaction scenario consistent with the observational evidence. Using deep VLT/FORS imaging in the $V$ and $I$ bands, we select GC candidates around the cluster centre based on morphology and colour criteria. We use a Voronoi tessellation approach to study the GC spatial distribution and density profiles, demonstrating its advantages over traditional annuli-based methods. We then constrain the evolution of the galaxy luminosity function using the GC specific frequency and colour distribution. The GC spatial distribution shows significant substructure. As in other clusters, red GCs are concentrated on the brightest galaxies, whereas blue GCs trace a more extended structure. We confirm and quantify the previously reported 'bridge' of GCs connecting NGC 4696 and NGC 4709, showing that it hosts twice as many GCs as expected from the projected GC systems of the two galaxies alone. The inferred evolution of the galaxy luminosity function agrees with previous results for Hydra I, yielding a faint-end slope of $α=-1.87_{-0.06}^{+0.06}$ for the galaxy population that assembled the Centaurus core. We propose a nearly head-on collision scenario in which Cen 45 recently passed through Cen 30, with the two subclusters moving approximately along the line of sight. This scenario explains the large line-of-sight velocity difference between the subclusters, the hot X-ray-emitting gas between them, the GC bridge, and the puffed-up GC system of NGC 4709.

Method: 采用机器学习双谐（ML-DH）框架，将神经网络力场与电子偶极模型相结合，在远低于常规量子化学计算成本的前提下达到量子化学精度，并已通过独立DFT计算和基准光谱数据库验证。

Conclusion: 该光谱库可为现有参考库提供补充，用于解释和建模JWST AIB观测及未来红外任务的数据。

Abstract: The interpretation of interstellar aromatic infrared bands (AIBs) relies on reference spectral libraries, but the chemical space covered by existing databases remains incomplete relative to the vast structural diversity of polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons (PAHs) expected in the interstellar medium. We present a large-scale, high-throughput PAH infrared (IR) spectral database that complements existing reference libraries by providing significantly more continuous coverage of the PAH chemical space for small to medium-sized PAHs. We used a machine-learning double-harmonic (ML-DH) framework that integrates neural network force fields with electronic dipole models. This framework achieves quantum-chemical accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost. It has been validated against independent density functional theory calculations and benchmark spectral databases. The resulting dataset comprises 144,111 IR spectra for 48,037 closed-shell, even-carbon benzenoid PAH structures across neutral, cationic, and anionic charge states, complete with per-spectrum uncertainty estimates. The spectral library, optimized geometries, and model code are publicly available. They can be used to complement existing libraries for interpreting and modeling JWST AIB observations and those from future IR missions.

</details>

Method: 从 ATOMS 巡天选取 139 个大质量原星团，用 ALMA 在 1.3 mm 波段以约 0.3″ 分辨率观测（中位距离约 3.8 kpc，对应约 10^3 au）；使用 getsf 识别 3374 个致密核，依据谱线诊断（H30α、CH3CN、CH3OH 等）与 CO 外流关联将其分为无星核、原恒星核、热核及 H II 区候选，并统计分析核质量函数、核形成效率与分子示踪物探测率。

Conclusion: 该巡天提供了大质量原星团中致密核的大样本目录，揭示核质量函数测量受距离效应影响、核形成效率随演化提高，并强调环境在核化学演化中的关键作用。

Abstract: The ALMA-QUARKS (Querying Underlying Mechanisms of Massive Star Formation with ALMA-Resolved Gas Kinematics and Structures) survey targets 139 massive protoclusters selected from the ATOMS survey. The targets were observed at 1.3~mm wavelength with an angular resolution of $\sim0.3^{\prime\prime}$, corresponding to $\sim10^3$~au at the sample's median distance of $\sim3.8$~kpc. Using \texttt{getsf}, we identify 3374 dense cores and classify them as starless, protostellar, hot core, or H{\sc ii} region candidates based on spectral-line diagnostics (e.g., H30$α$, CH$_3$CN, and CH$_3$OH) and CO outflow associations. The resulting catalog provides core physical properties and enables statistical investigations of dense core populations in massive protocluster environments. The derived global core mass function (CMF) after excluding H{\sc ii} region candidates, which is biased by distance effects, has a relatively flat power-law slope at the high-mass end ($\mathrm{d}N/\mathrm{d}\log M \propto M^{-γ}$, with $γ=0.98\pm0.03$). In contrast, the measured high-mass slopes in individual distance bins are approximately Salpeter-like, while their fitted lower mass limits increase with distance. We further examine the core formation efficiency (CFE) across the full sample and find that it positively correlates with the luminosity-to-mass ratio of the parental clumps, consistent with an evolutionary increase in the fraction of mass concentrated into dense cores. We also investigate the detection rates of different molecular tracers and find systematic chemical variations with evolutionary stage. Compared with the ASHES survey, we find that the detection rates of deuterated species vary significantly even for cores at the same evolutionary stages, suggesting an important environmental influence on the chemical evolution of cores.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮早型星系（ETGs）在近邻星系团中数量丰富，是研究环境驱动星系转变的关键实验室。深成像揭示部分矮ETGs存在内嵌螺旋状特征（如棒和旋臂），但其在矮星系尺度上的起源仍不确定，因此需要检验这些特征是否与周围弥散成分存在差异以约束形成路径。

Result: 螺旋状特征仅贡献总光的一小部分（i波段约1.5-5.5%，g波段约1.7-3.7%）。样本颜色多样：4个星系的螺旋状特征比臂间区更红，4个无稳健的臂-臂间颜色差异，1个螺旋状成分明显更蓝。后者与近期淬灭的过渡天体一致，而偏红和颜色均匀的特征则与更年老或更富金属的星族一致。

Conclusion: 室女座矮ETGs的螺旋状特征在测光性质上表现出惊人的多样性，暗示存在多种演化通道而非单一机制。该试点分析也表明，已具备对更大规模NGVS中潜在含螺旋状特征矮星系样本开展研究的条件。

Abstract: Dwarf early-type galaxies (ETGs) are abundant in nearby clusters and provide a key laboratory for studying environmentally driven galaxy transformation. Although dwarf ETGs were once considered structurally simple, deep imaging has revealed embedded spiral-like features (e.g., bars and spiral arms) in some of them, contributing only a small fraction of the total light. Their origin at dwarf scales remains uncertain. As a pilot study, we investigate spiral-like features in nine Virgo dwarf ETGs with clear embedded arms, using optical colors to test whether these features differ from the surrounding diffuse component, which would constrain their formation pathways. Using deep g- and i-band NGVS images, we apply a residual-based method to separate excess light associated with the spiral-like component from the diffuse host. The residual images define arm and interarm regions, enabling direct measurements of light fractions and g-i colors for both components. The spiral-like features contribute only a few percent of the total light (about 1.5-5.5% in i and 1.7-3.7% in g). We find a diverse range of colors across the sample: four galaxies host spiral-like features redder than their interarm regions, four show no robust arm-interarm color difference, and one exhibits a distinctly bluer spiral-like component. The latter is consistent with a recently quenched transition object, while the redder and color-uniform features are consistent with older or more metal-rich stellar populations. Spiral-like features in Virgo dwarf ETGs thus exhibit a surprising diversity in their photometric properties, implying multiple evolutionary channels rather than a single one. This pilot analysis also demonstrates readiness for the much larger NGVS sample of dwarfs with potential spiral-like features.

Abstract: We present a galaxy group catalog built from Data Release 1 of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) Bright Galaxy Survey, using the Self Calibrated Group Finder. The algorithm uses clustering data from the observed sample to calibrate the Group Finder's free parameters, rather than simulations. The catalog contains 2,229,741 galaxies from ~$2680$ deg$^2$ of sky with 3-pass coverage and $r$-band fluxes $\lt 19.5$. The catalog classifies all galaxies in the flux-limited sample as centrals or satellites and estimates host dark matter halo masses. We show the satellite fraction $f_{\rm sat}$, luminosity halo mass relation, stellar halo mass relation and halo occupation distributions (HODs) from the catalog. We find these non-parametric HODs can be fit by standard HOD models, with satellite power-law index $α$ values around 0.75 for star-forming galaxies and around 1.1 for quiescent objects. We resolve the infrared nebula on spatial scales of ${\sim}15-25$\,pc and report the first detections of [Ne\,{\sc v}] and [Ne\,{\sc vi}] in this source. We find anisotropic ionizing conditions: emission from the highest-ionization species arises from compact regions offset from the ULX toward the previously detected radio knots, whereas lower-ionization species trace the wider nebula. Spectral lines across the nebula are narrow and consistent with the instrumental resolution, indicating kinematically quiescent gas and disfavoring shocks as the dominant ionization mechanism. Conclusion: 限制窗口模型可从直接光谱特征中恢复丰度，全谱模型在诊断特征微弱或缺失时可利用与其他标签的相关性，二者具有互补优势。该方法证明了低分辨率、低信噪比恒星光谱在构建河外星系考古学均质数据集方面的潜力，有助于更好地理解本星系群的化学富集多样性。

Abstract: Element abundances in nearby galaxies provide insights into their chemical enrichment histories and place the Milky Way and its neighbours in a broader cosmological context. These systems can be studied through individual stars, whose resolved spectra enable the construction of a chemical abundance census of the Local Group. We present the second stage of the ACACIAS (extra-galactic ArChAeology with Cannon Inferred Abundances from resolved integral field unit Spectra) program, which aims to build the first homogeneous data set of stellar abundances across dwarf galaxies spanning a range of masses for comparison with Milky Way properties. We analyse low-resolution (R=1800), low-SNR (signal-to-noise ratio, ~ 25) MUSE spectra for 39 stars in the Sculptor dwarf spheroidal galaxy. Using the data-driven method The Cannon, we combine high-fidelity labels from the Apache Point Observatory Galactic Evolution Experiment (APOGEE) survey with MUSE-like Large Sky Area Multi-Object Fiber Spectroscopic Telescope (LAMOST) spectra to analyse the MUSE data. This enables the determination of [Mg/Fe], [Mn/Fe], [C/Fe], [N/Fe], and [Al/Fe] on the APOGEE abundance scale, with precisions of σ = 0.09-0.37 dex. Comparing model configurations, we show that [Mg/Fe], [Mn/Fe], and [C/Fe] are best recovered from targeted spectral windows, whereas stellar parameters and [Al/Fe] benefit from full-spectrum information with telluric masking. This reflects the complementary strengths of restricted-window models, which recover abundances from direct spectral features, and full-spectrum models, which exploit correlations with other labels when diagnostic features are weak or absent. Our approach demonstrates the potential of low-resolution, low-SNR stellar spectra to build homogeneous datasets for extragalactic archaeology to better understand the chemical enrichment diversity of the Local Group.

The data achieved a spatial resolution of 48 pc in the southwestern bar-end region of M83, covering an area of 2.1 kpc x 1.8 kpc. However, in regions with high column densities, N2H+ becomes a super-linear function of HCN, suggesting that the N2H+ emission is more sensitive to dense gas than HCN. We employed a radiative transfer model that predicts line intensities as a function of column density, adopting parametric column density probability distribution functions (N-PDFs) to describe the underlying column density distribution. The model demonstrates that the observed linear or superlinear trend can be explained by a log-normal distribution only or a log-normal and a power-law tail distribution in the N-PDF. Furthermore, the N2H+ intensity is significantly more sensitive to the power-law tail than the HCN intensity. In addition, our analysis incorporating archival data shows that N2H+ is a less reliable tracer of dense gas in galactic center regions, where warmer temperatures may promote N2H+ destruction.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矮椭球星系几乎探测不到冷气体，但其气体损失中恒星反馈、再电离与环境效应各自的相对作用仍不确定，需要一种简单的能量判据来评估恒星反馈是否有足够的累积能量。

Conclusion: 该检验区分了"累积反馈能量是否充足"与"决定淬灭时序的过程"这两个不同问题，τ_E是能量诊断量而非风驱动发射的直接测量。

Abstract: Dwarf spheroidal galaxies (dSphs) contain little detectable cold gas, but the relative roles of stellar feedback, reionization, and environmental effects in their gas loss remain uncertain. We apply an order-of-magnitude energy-budget test to nine Milky Way dwarf satellites using published color--magnitude-diagram star formation histories (SFHs), galaxy-level velocity dispersions, effective radii, and integrated $V$-band luminosities. For each galaxy and halo prescription, we define the energy-equivalence time $τ_{\mathrm{E}}$, when a crossing exists, as the first elapsed time at which the cumulative effective energy from core-collapse supernovae equals the adopted gas binding energy. This quantity is an energetic diagnostic rather than a direct measurement of wind launch. Under the fiducial parameters, all nine galaxies reach the nominal threshold under the kinematic--size prescription K+B16, yet star formation often continues long after threshold crossing. We compare the corresponding lookback time $t_{\mathrm{E,lb}}$ with $t_{90,\mathrm{lb}}$, the lookback time at which 90\% of the total initial stellar mass had formed. Because both times are derived from the same cumulative SFH, their temporal proximity is interpreted descriptively rather than as independent evidence of feedback-driven quenching. Whether the threshold is reached depends on the adopted halo mass, radius, retained gas fraction, and supernova--gas coupling efficiency. These results distinguish the availability of sufficient cumulative feedback energy from the processes that determine the timing of quenching.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究星系盘重定向（盘倾斜）现象及其与并合事件的关联，理解并合如何改变盘的自旋取向，以及重定向过程对盘结构稳定性的影响。

Method: 从TNG50宇宙学模拟中选取93个银河质量星系，识别出117次具有明确起止时间的盘倾斜事件，按盘演化是否平滑（平滑演化 vs. 快速破坏与重构）分为两类，并将每次倾斜事件与时间上最接近的最大质量并合事件关联，重点分析平滑演化阶段的倾斜事件。

Result: 平滑演化阶段的倾斜速率通常低于10°/Gyr，持续2-9 Gyr，多与小质量并合相关；约40%事件倾角超过30°，触发伴星系质量不超过星系总恒星质量，多沿切向、逆行或近垂直轨道并合。盘倾向于向伴星系轨道自旋方向对齐，逆行轨道可导致大倾角甚至盘与轨道面近正交的中间态。因倾斜速率较慢，恒星盘保持稳定并持续增长，经历强倾斜的星系盘厚度与无明显重定向的星系相当。

Conclusion: 盘倾斜主要由小质量伴星系的切向/逆行并合驱动，过程缓慢且不破坏恒星盘，星系即使经历强烈重定向仍可维持薄盘结构。

Abstract: We investigated disk reorientation and its relationship to mergers in a sample of 93 Milky Way-mass galaxies drawn from the TNG50 simulations. From this sample, we identified 117 individual disk-tilt events with clearly defined start and end times, and we divided them into two classes based on whether the disk evolves smoothly or undergoes rapid disruption and regrowth during the tilt duration. For each tilt event, we linked the most massive merger that occurs in close temporal association with it. We focus on those tilt events occurring during phases of smooth disk evolution, which typically show tilt rates below 10 degrees per Gyr, persist for 2-9 Gyr, and are generally connected to minor mergers. Roughly 40% of these events reach tilt angles exceeding 30 degrees, most often triggered by satellites whose masses are comparable to or less massive than the galaxy's total stellar mass, merging on tangential orbits with orbital angular momentum that is retrograde or nearly perpendicular to the disk spin. The satellite consistently drives the disk tilt toward alignment with the satellite's orbital spin; satellites that came in on retrograde orbits relative to the disk spin can thus induce large tilt angles and can produce an almost orthogonal configuration between the stellar disk and the satellite's orbital plane as an intermediate phase. Owing to the comparatively slow tilt rates, the stellar disk can keep stable and continue to grow throughout the reorientation process. Consequently, galaxies that experience strong disk tilts can still maintain stellar disks that are as thin as those in systems without significant reorientation.

</details>


### [19] [Are High-EW LAEs good analogs for the earliest galaxies? A study on the ionizing conditions and gas properties of LAEs at $2\lesssim z\lesssim7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21232)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lyα发射体（LAEs）常被视为驱动宇宙再电离的低质量、贫金属星系类似物，尤其是大Lyα等值宽（EW）的星系被认为尚未经历显著化学增丰。作者希望直接检验这一假说，即EW是否是金属丰度（化学演化程度）的可靠指标。

Result: 88个LAEs中仅50个在深NIRCam成像中有可靠对应体，凸显该群体的本质暗弱；最终仅16个源同时具备可靠恒星质量和气相金属丰度。这些源在诊断图中符合低金属丰度恒星形成系统。强线金属丰度诊断R3随EW增大而下降，但基于全发射线拟合时，EW与气相金属丰度之间无强单调关系。不过，Lyα发射更强的星系在固定恒星质量下倾向于位于质量-金属丰度关系之下，表明它们比同质量典型星系化学演化程度更低。

Conclusion: 高EW的LAEs可作为驱动宇宙再电离星系的"有用但不完美"的类似物；将Lyα等值宽与恒星质量结合，可能是识别最具化学原始性星系更为有效的途径。

Abstract: Lyman-$α$ emitters (LAEs) are frequently proposed as analogs of the low-mass, metal-poor galaxies thought to dominate cosmic reionization. In particular, galaxies with large Ly$α$ equivalent widths (EWs) are expected to have not experienced significant chemical enrichment. We test this hypothesis using a sample of 88 LAEs at $2 \lesssim z\lesssim 7$, selected from MUSE observations that are undetected in deep HST imaging and subsequently followed up with JWST/NIRSpec spectroscopy. Despite the unprecedented sensitivity of JWST, only 50 of the LAEs exhibit secure counterparts in available deep NIRCam imaging reaching typical 3$σ$ depths of $m_\text{AB}\sim30.3$ mag, highlighting the intrinsically faint nature of the population. Gas-phase metallicities are inferred by fitting optical emission lines with a grid of BPASS+cloudy photoionization models, while stellar masses are derived from NIRCam photometry through SED fitting. The final sample with secure stellar masses and gas-phase metallicities encompasses only 16 LAEs, occupying regions of standard emission-line diagnostic diagrams consistent with low-metallicity star-forming systems. Although the strong-line metallicity diagnostic R3 appears to decrease with increasing EW, we find no compelling evidence for a strong monotonic relation between EW and gas-phase metallicity when metallicities are inferred through full emission-line fitting. However, galaxies with stronger Ly$α$ emission tend to lie below the average mass-metallicity relation at fixed stellar masses, suggesting that they are preferentially less chemically evolved than typical galaxies of the same mass. We conclude that high-EW LAEs provide useful, though imperfect, analogs of the galaxies thought to drive cosmic reionization, and combining EW with stellar mass may provide a more effective route for identifying the most chemically primitive galaxies.

*J. A. Gray,H. R. Russell,F. Combes,O. Almaini,P. E. J. Nulsen,A. C. Fabian,B. R. McNamara,T. Rose,P. Salomé*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 发现单独任一项（改引力或改惯性）都不能导致MOND现象，只有两者联合才可。展示了二者如何结合产生主要MOND预言：转动曲线的渐近平坦性、质量-渐近速度关系、深MOND的位力质量-速度弥散(M-σ)关系。此外，运动学方程与引力场方程中出现不同的插值函数，各自适用于不同情形，且都不具备标准深MOND极限。

Abstract: It is plausible that in a more fundamental theory of MOND both gravity and inertia will differ from their Newtonian versions. Such theories, if they embody the basic tenets of MOND, will share its primary predictions with existing theories, which modify either gravity or inertia. But they are expected to differ in their structure and in their secondary predictions. Here, I discuss families of nonrelativistic MOND theories that modify both the kinetic and the gravitational actions of Newtonian dynamics. Each family is spanned by one parameter $0\leη\le 1$, which is the scaling dimension of the deep-MOND limit of all terms in the action. Pure modified gravity ($η=0$) and pure modified inertia ($η=1$) are the end cases in each family. I speak of families, since the modified action terms themselves are not strictly specified, but are treated in rather general terms. These theories are artificial in that they are not based on a single unifying idea that dictates the modification of the two components. But they are still useful in pinpointing some idiosyncracies of such theories. For example, neither of the two modified terms, by itself, leads to MOND phenomenology; only their combined effect does. I show how the two combine to make some of the primary MOND predictions, such as asymptotic flatness of rotation curves, the mass-asymptotic-speed relation, and the deep-MOND, virial, mass-velocity-dispersion ($M-σ$) relation. Another idiosyncracy of such theories is the emergence of different interpolating functions in the kinetic equations of motion and in the gravitational field equations, each relevant under different circumstances, and neither of which have the standard deep-MOND limit. Such examples alert us to the wide range of theories that can lead to MOND phenomenology.

Abstract: The conversion of cold dense gas, traced by molecular line emission, into stars remains one of the fundamental unsolved problems in galaxy evolution. Observations over the past decades have established empirical relations between gas and star formation, yet their physical origin remains uncertain. Gravity, turbulence, and stellar feedback as main drivers provide fundamentally different pictures of how star formation is regulated, while reproducing remarkably similar galaxy-scale scaling relations. To disentangle the individual importance of each process, not only its mass and distribution, but also its temperature, density, excitation, and chemistry must be probed. However, current or planned (sub)millimetre facilities cannot combine statistical breadth, cloud-scale resolution, and multi-line diagnostics across large galaxy samples. A new, flagship interferometer with increased line sensitivity and bandwidth enables obtaining multi-transition, multi-isotopologue observations of molecular gas at GMC-resolved scales across nearby galaxies, enabling millions of independent measurements of its physical state across a wide range of extragalactic environments. This will transform molecular gas from an empirical tracer into a quantitative probe of the physical processes governing the relation between the dense gas and star formation. With the 2040s landscape of optical and radio facilities, ALMA2040 will facilitate a predictive theory of the conversion of gas into stars.

This algorithm provides a reliable, general-purpose tool for precise measurement of interstellar features in time-domain spectroscopic surveys.

These percentages are computed relative to all galaxies in each mass division, including those not searchable for AGN, with numerical ranges representing large systematics between crossmatched emission-line measurements from different SDSS catalogs. WISE AGN are detected in $<$1\% of galaxies at all masses with optimized WISE photometry. We show that standard WISE catalog photometry yields high rates of both false positives and false negatives. AGN demographics shift with mass due to correlations with gas content, star formation, and group environment, forming a continuum: SF-AGN at the high-SF end, then ``Bonus AGN'' (defined by alternate literature BPT demarcations), then Composites, then Conventional AGN at the low-SF end. Typical (metal-poor, star-forming) dwarfs most often host SF-AGN, whereas typical transitional galaxies most often host Bonus AGN and Composites. Conclusion: 通过预先市场承诺将AI产业的数据需求与滞留学生科学任务对接，可在不占用联邦拨款的前提下使更多低成本科学任务得以飞行，从而缓解科学任务"选得上、飞不了"的结构性困境。

Abstract: Every year NASA validates more excellent science missions than it can afford to fly. Some of the most promising missions in astrophysics, heliophysics, and planetary science are judged selectable, yet remain grounded indefinitely. Mission Market is an advance market commitment that can fly lower cost missions without competing for federal appropriations. Funding comes primarily from the enormous capital now flowing into artificial intelligence (AI) companies that value generating frontier data for scientific foundation models and is also supplemented by traditional means such as philanthropy and patron-scientists.

</details>

*Yasmin Cavalcante-Coelho,Karín Menéndez-Delmestre,Taehyun Kim,Arianna Cortesi,Natan de Isídio,Thiago S. Gonçalves,Kartik Sheth,Osbaldo Sánchez-García,Marco Grossi,Thiago Bueno-Dalpiaz*

Result: 最简构型（盘+棒）通常出现在低质量系统；质量更大、具有显著核球的星系拥有更重、更高的棒质量比。核球的Sérsic指数多低于2，符合核恒星盘的测光特征。在固定星系质量下，棒的相对长度呈双峰分布，有核球的星系其棒相对更长。

Abstract: Bars are stellar structures commonly found in disk galaxies and important agents of secular evolution. Quantifying the stellar mass content of bars provides a physically motivated approach to assess how bar growth depends on galaxy mass and internal structure. We perform a GALFITM multi-band mid-infrared two-dimensional decomposition of 344 barred galaxies from the Spitzer Survey of Stellar Structure in Galaxies ($S^4G$) in the 3.6 and 4.5 $μ$m bands. We estimate the stellar mass associated with each structural component (i.e., disk, bulge, bar, and nuclear point source) and investigate how bar properties correlate with global galaxy characteristics. Our final models consist of 2-4 components per galaxy, with the simplest configuration (disk + bar) typically associated with low-mass systems. We find that more massive galaxies with prominent bulges host more massive bars and higher median bar-to-total stellar mass ratios. These bulges generally present Sérsic indices below 2, consistent with a photometric signature of nuclear stellar disks. Additionally, when we consider the relative bar lengths at a fixed galaxy mass, we find a bimodal distribution where galaxies with bulges host relatively longer bars compared to their bulgeless counterparts. These findings could be the result of different evolutionary paths, possibly linked to different disk formation epochs, allowing bars more time to grow in size and mass. Our study indicates that bar evolution may follow distinct pathways across different galaxy mass regimes, and is likely influenced by secular processes and environmental effects.

Abstract: Dark matter constitutes most of the matter in the Universe, yet its fundamental nature and distribution remain poorly understood. Stellar streams, formed through the tidal disruption of globular clusters and dwarf galaxies, provide powerful tracers of the Milky Way's gravitational potential and dark matter halo. In this review, we examine how stellar stream morphology and kinematics can constrain the halo's structure, shape, and substructure. We discuss key observational signatures, including stream gaps, spurs, and perturbations, and investigate five prominent streams: Sagittarius, GD-1, Palomar 5, Orphan-Chenab, and Cetus Polar. We summarize analytical, statistical, and Bayesian methods used to model stellar streams and infer dark matter properties, highlighting challenges arising from parameter degeneracies, incomplete observations, progenitor uncertainties, and time-dependent perturbations, particularly those associated with the Large Magellanic Cloud. Finally, we discuss how upcoming astrometric and photometric surveys, together with improved dynamical modelling, may strengthen constraints on the Milky Way's dark matter distribution and help distinguish between cold, warm, and self-interacting dark matter scenarios.

Abstract: Observations of debris disks provide a valuable window to investigate the formation and evolution of planetary systems. We present high-contrast JWST/NIRCam observations of CPD-72 2713, a 24 Myr-old star that belongs to the $β$ Pic moving group. We obtained the first scattered-light images of the disk in both F210M and F444W filters, revealing a nearly face-on orientation. Forward modeling yields disk radii of $\rm 55.4^{+3.0}_{-3.2}\,AU$ (F210M) and $\rm 39.1^{+4.6}_ {-2.6}\,AU$ (F444W), both significantly smaller than the thermal emission radii measured with ALMA and Herschel/PACS. This discrepancy suggests that scattered light traces the inner region of the disk while thermal emission probes the outer regions. Combining the stellar rotation period ($4.47\pm0.67$ days) from TESS light curves and spectroscopic $v\sin i_*$, we constrained the stellar inclination to $47^{\circ}.2^{+11.1}_{-7.7}$ ($1σ$). The minimum star-disk mutual inclinations are $27^{\circ}.6 _{-8.2} ^{+11.5}\ (1σ)$ from F210M data and $27^{\circ}.3 _{-10.9}^{ +12.5}\ (1σ)$ in F444W data. We find that the minimum star--disk mutual inclination exceeds $10^\circ$ at the $95\%$ credible level in both F210M and F444W, suggesting a misalignment between the scattered-light disk and the stellar equatorial plane. We note that variation among published disk inclination measurements and potential systematic uncertainties may affect this probability. With JWST high-contrast imaging, we rule out planets down to $0.3\ \rm M_{J}$ at $0.5^{\prime\prime}$ and beyond, disfavoring a planet as the driver of the star-disk misalignment.

### [32] [Orbital Stability of Apsidally Aligned Planetary Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21117)
*Gabriel Teixeira Guimarães,Eiichiro Kokubo*

Main category: astro-ph.EP

TL;DR: 紧邻行星系统的长期动力学稳定性不能仅用轨道偏心率来判断，还取决于拱线共振（拱线对齐与一致进动）是否得以维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧邻行星系统的动力学稳定性被认为强烈依赖轨道偏心率，但拱线对齐（apsidal alignment）的物理作用尚不清楚，缺乏明确的稳定性判据，因此需要系统性研究偏心率不同成分对稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用直接 N 体模拟，将行星偏心率分解为两个成分：所有行星共有的系统性成分（systematic eccentricity）与决定拱线对齐程度的随机成分（random eccentricity），并比较二者对不稳定时标的影响。

Result: 随机偏心率使相邻行星对进入平运动共振重叠区，驱动混沌演化、降低稳定性；系统性偏心率在很大取值范围内对不稳定时标影响很小，因为行星保持拱线对齐和近乎固定的相对轨道取向。拱线对齐系统几乎与初始圆轨道系统一样稳定。但该保护机制存在有限适用范围：超过某个临界系统性偏心率后，一致的拱线进动被破坏，出现差异进动，不稳定时标迅速下降。

Conclusion: 紧邻行星系统的长期稳定性不能仅由轨道偏心率刻画，还取决于一致拱线进动的维持，这为解析稳定性判据提供了新方向。

Abstract: The dynamical stability of compact planetary systems is known to depend strongly on orbital eccentricities, but the physical role of apsidal alignment remains poorly understood. We investigate this problem using direct {\em N}-body simulations by separating planetary eccentricities into a systematic component shared by all planets and a random component that determines the degree of apsidal alignment. We find that these two components play fundamentally different dynamical roles. Random eccentricity destabilizes planetary systems by driving neighboring planet pairs into the regime of mean-motion resonance overlap, leading to chaotic evolution. In contrast, systematic eccentricity has little effect on the instability timescale over a wide range of values because the planetary orbits remain in apsidal alignment, preserving nearly fixed relative orbital orientations despite finite eccentricities. As a result, systems that start in apsidal alignment are nearly as stable as initially circular systems. This protection mechanism has a finite domain of validity. Beyond a critical systematic eccentricity, coherent apsidal precession breaks down, differential precession develops, and the instability timescale decreases rapidly. Our results demonstrate that the long-term stability of compact planetary systems cannot be characterized solely by orbital eccentricities but also depends on the maintenance of coherent apsidal precession, providing a new direction for analytical stability criteria.

</details>

Abstract: We present Zwicky Transient Facility observations of the interstellar comet 3I/ATLAS to characterize its dust-activity evolution before and after perihelion. Before perihelion, the effective scattering cross section $C_{\rm e}$ brightened across the inner $2\times10^4$ km as $C_{\rm e}\propto r_{\rm H}^{-γ}$, with $γ$ increasing from $1.63\pm0.14$ to $1.88\pm0.34$ with aperture size. After perihelion, the heliocentric index decreased from $2.44\pm0.09$ in the $5\times10^3$ km aperture to $1.54\pm0.09$ in the $8\times10^4$ km aperture, indicating faster fading of the inner coma. Under adopted grain-size and residence-time assumptions, the implied dust mass-loss rates are $\sim123$--$203~{\rm kg~s^{-1}}$ before perihelion and $\sim596$--$1412~{\rm kg~s^{-1}}$ after perihelion. The morphology yields a sunward ejection-speed scale of $v_{\rm ej}\sim(190\pm3)a_{μ{\rm m}}^{-1/2}~{\rm m~s^{-1}}$ and an effective perpendicular velocity of $v_\perp=(79\pm6)a_{μ{\rm m}}^{-1/2}~{\rm m~s^{-1}}$. Post-perihelion surface-brightness profiles transiently steepened to $k\sim-2.7$ in late November and early December, then became shallower and approached $k=-1.5$ by January. Matched $g-r$ profiles show that this phase coincided with an anomalously blue outer coma that disappeared by January. We characterize the fading component with a projected destruction length of $\ell_{\rm DE}\sim(6.4$--$7.2)\times10^4$ km. Thermophysical sublimation calculations indicate that this scale, together with the order of magnitude of the water production near the main imaging epoch, is consistent with $a_0\sim5$--$10~μ{\rm m}$ icy grains containing a small absorbing carbonaceous fraction of $f_{\rm C}\sim0.1\%$. These results suggest that the early post-perihelion coma likely contained a short-lived, volatile-bearing icy-grain component whose fading contributed to the outer-coma brightness and color evolution

Abstract: We analyze the post-perihelion coma of the Great Comet of 2024, C/2023 A3 (Tsuchinshan--ATLAS) to characterize dust activity and assess the contribution of sublimating icy grains to a candidate near-nucleus halo. Using archival Zwicky Transient Facility images from 15 February to 26 May 2025, we performed aperture photometry within a fixed projected radius of 40,000 km and derived azimuthally averaged surface-brightness profiles. The comet faded from 12.86 to 14.77 mag as heliocentric distance increased from 2.64 to 3.90 au, with two short-lived brightenings. The effective dust cross section decreased by a factor of 2.4, following $C_{\rm e}\propto r_{\rm h}^{-3.1}$, while the dust mass production rate scaled as $\dot{M}_{\rm d}\propto r_{\rm h}^{-3.41}$, consistent with a contribution from water-ice-driven activity. Extrapolating the fitted relation to perihelion implies a model-dependent cumulative dust mass loss of $3.0\times10^{11}$ kg, equivalent to a globally averaged erosion depth of $\sim1.4$ m for an assumed nucleus radius of 5.9 km. Coma profiles were shallower than the canonical steady-state expectation, suggesting radial changes in dust distribution or scattering properties, possibly due to delayed aggregate fragmentation. A persistent central brightness excess, coincident with less-red inner-coma colors, is consistent with an icy-grain halo. Among the models explored, icy grains of radius $\sim10~μ$m with carbon fraction $\sim0.1\%$ yield halo radii comparable to those observed during enhanced activity on 21--25 February 2025, with grain lifetimes of $2.8\times10^5$--$3.2\times10^5$ s. No single fixed grain size and composition reproduces the full halo evolution; an increasing carbon fraction could explain the broad halo-radius trend, possibly reflecting progressive refractory enrichment of near-surface material.

Abstract: Circumplanetary disks (CPDs) play an important role in the formation of gas giants and their satellites, yet remain poorly understood owing to the lack of observables and comprehensive theoretical models. Incorporating $α$-viscosity, a free-fall prescription of mass infall and boundary layer accretion, we develop an axisymmetric viscous radiation hydrodynamic (RHD) model. We evolve this model for different physical parameters using the RHD code {\tt Guangqi}, which solves RHD equations under the flux-limited diffusion approximation. Overall, disk thickness increases with accretion rate and decreases with planet mass, consistent with the expected trend in conventional $α$-disk models. On the other hand, we highlight that the boundary layer plays a significant role in setting angular momentum transport and thermodynamics of the inner CPD, yielding ``hot'' ($s_{\rm real}>11\,k_{\rm{B}}\,m_{\rm{H}}^{-1}$) accreted gas, and potentially leading to dust sublimation. We identify a supersonic accretion flow, amounting to $2\%$--$5\%$ of the total accretion rate, located at high elevations above the CPDs with low angular momentum in our simulations. If the planet is massive ($\gtrsim3\,M_{\rm J}$), this low angular momentum flow impacting the planetary surface could generate observable H$α$ emission when the shock accretion rate is $\gtrsim5.5\times10^{-8}\, M_{\rm J}\,\rm{yr}^{-1}$.

We also analyzed the medium resolution GRAVITY+ K band spectrum in combination with new VLT/SPHERE and literature photometry, exploring both the atmospheric properties and the constraints on the circumplanetary environment. Finally, we checked for any signal from a resolved circumplanetary disk in the GRAVITY+ data. We detected the protoplanet WISPIT 2 b at $\sim$322 mas separation from its host star. While the GRAVITY+ observations do also not resolve a circumplanetary disk, we place an upper limit on the possible spatial extent of such a disk. The new GRAVITY+ observations independently confirm WISPIT 2 b as a self-luminous and point-like companion residing inside the gap of the transitional disk at $\sim$60 au from its host star. Abstract: Understanding the origin and evolution of planetary atmospheres, and the connection between their lower and upper atmospheric layers, remains a fundamental challenge in exoplanet science. Addressing these questions requires observations spanning a broad wavelength range, from the ultraviolet to the near-infrared, together with the ability to simultaneously characterise planetary and stellar variability. The Wide-band Atmospheric Laboratory for Transiting Exoplanet Research (WALTzER) is an ESA F3 mission candidate designed to address this need through simultaneous low-resolution spectroscopy and high-precision photometry of transiting exoplanets. WALTzER covers simultaneously the near-ultraviolet (NUV: 240--325 nm) and visible (VIS: 450--820 nm) bands with two medium resolution spectrographs and the near-infrared (NIR: 900--1700 nm) range with a broadband photometer. We outline the main science drivers and present the mission concept and instrument design. WALTzER's broad, simultaneous wavelength coverage enables the detection and characterisation of the atmospheres of more than 30 gas-rich exoplanets and provides data relevant for a broad range of Solar System and Galactic astrophysics science cases. The payload consists of a 35 cm Cassegrain telescope feeding three co-aligned channels (i.e. NUV, VIS, NIR), sharing a common optical train to minimise instrumental systematics, while maximising observational efficiency.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌行星系统演化存在近距离交会、散射、碰撞等小时间尺度过程。传统模拟对所有粒子使用固定步长，效率低且浪费算力，还可能欠解析；混合方法过于粗糙，无法细粒度自适应；而为天体分配随时间变化的独立步长通常会破坏辛性与可逆性等理想性质。

Method: 开发"近乎"精确时间可逆的直接N体积分器MTR：为每对行星分配各自的步长，步长随系统演化而变化，且这种自适应是实时进行的，不依赖全局步长。提供C版本代码并在多个问题上测试。

Result: 测试显示无能量误差漂移，符合可逆方法预期。在粒子数高达N=975时与最先进的GPLUM对比：相比其Hermite直接模式提速最高达两个数量级，且轨道分布一致；在默认配置下，MTR速度与tree模式相当或更优，最高提升一个数量级，尽管tree模式使用更快但近似的力计算。

Conclusion: MTR以逐对、随时间可变且实时自适应的步长，实现了近乎精确可逆的直接N体积分，在保持轨道统计一致的同时显著提升大粒子数混沌系统模拟的效率，优于现有先进代码的直接与tree模式。

Abstract: Simulating chaotic planetary system evolution requires resolving small time-scales due to close encounters, scattering, collisions, and more. Simulations typically assign fixed timesteps to all particles, however, which is inefficient and wastes compute resources in a chaotically evolving system; moreover, it may under-resolve the simulation. Hybrid methods are a blunt tool for resolving small time-scales as they do not allow for fine-grained adaptation. Assigning individual timesteps, which vary in time, to planetary bodies usually violates desirable properties such as symplecticity and reversibility. In this work, we develop an "almost" exactly time-reversible direct $N$-body integrator, MTR, in which each pair of planets gets its own timestep, which itself can vary as the system evolves. In contrast to our previous work, we achieve this adaptation on the fly, independent of a global timestep. We provide a C version of the code and test it on several problems, demonstrating no energy error drift, as expected of a reversible method. At large particle numbers, up to $N = 975$, we compare against the state-of-the-art GPLUM code, which we can use in direct Hermite mode (for which it is not optimized) or tree mode (for which it is). MTR provides a speedup of up to two orders of magnitude compared to the Hermite mode, while agreeing with the distribution of orbits. In our default configuration, MTR's speed matches or improves on the tree mode's speed, by up to an order magnitude, despite the tree mode's faster and approximate force calculation.

</details>

Abstract: We present a homogeneous analysis of 268 confirmed and candidate transiting hot-Jupiter host stars with 4800K < Teff < 6200K. We combine high-resolution TRES spectra, Gaia, 2MASS and WISE photometry, and Gaia astrometry to determine these stars' Teff, log g, Rstar, [alpha/Fe] and [Fe/H]. Based on their [alpha/Fe] and [Fe/H], we classify the stars as members of the Milky Way's low-[alpha/Fe] thin disk, high-[alpha/Fe] thick disk or an ambiguous intermediate population. We identify two confirmed hot Jupiters (WASP-81 b and TOI-5322 b) and three candidate hot Jupiters (TOI-3118.01, TOI-2728.01, and TOI-5781.01) as orbiting previously-unrecognized members of the high-alpha thick disk. We compare the properties of the confirmed planets in the three populations and note two indications of a potential difference between the populations: both high-[alpha/Fe] confirmed planets and eight out of the eleven ambiguous-disk confirmed planets are less massive than the median low-[alpha/Fe] planet, and none of our high-[alpha/Fe] or ambiguous confirmed planets have orbital periods shorter than 2.7 d, when 22% of our low-[alpha/Fe] population do. These differences may reflect variations in giant-planet formation across galactic environments, evolutionary effects, or differing stellar properties between the high- and low-[alpha/Fe] populations. Our catalog is the largest sample of chemically-identified high-[alpha/Fe] thick-disk confirmed and candidate hot-Jupiter host stars. These ancient systems, with ages > 8 Gyr, may allow for a better understanding of how the formation of hot Jupiters depends on galactic environment, and how their orbits evolve over very long time scales.

With a large $α_{\rm CO} \simeq 315 - 725\,M_\odot\,({\rm K\,km\,s}^{-1}\,{\rm pc}^2)^{-1}$ at a low gas-phase metallicity, the CO line would remain very challenging for the current ALMA to detect, while ALMA2040 will likely deliver a robust CO detection and thus a gas-reservoir constraint for The Egg. Assuming an optimistic, SMC-like $α_{\rm CO}$ with a 50-hour deep integration, we produce mock position-velocity diagrams that may distinguish a $10^7\,M_\odot$ black hole from the no-BH model through its steeper central velocity structure. We conclude that ALMA2040 has the potential to directly measure the gas masses, depletion times, and excitation of local LRDs, while the CO-bright subset may ultimately yield dynamical constraints on the mass of their central black holes.

Their properties reflect galactic disk conditions, and their evolution is shaped by stellar feedback and host-galaxy dynamics. Studying GMCs across cosmic time is therefore key to understanding how gas turns into stars and drives galaxy evolution. The combination of dramatically increased line sensitivity and wide instantaneous bandwidth will push the search for the first galaxies out to the earliest cosmic times, with [OIII] detectable up to $z\sim20$. At millimeter wavelengths, optically thick thermal emission provides a comparatively direct measure of gas temperature. Improved spatial resolution, enhanced uv-coverage, and simultaneous multi-band observations, as proposed for ALMA2040, would enable transformative progress in capturing dynamic processes across multiple heights and enable time-dependent three-dimensional thermal and magnetic tomography of the chromosphere. Abstract: In this report, we discuss the performance of the Walsh phase-switching scheme for Band 4 (550-750 MHz) polarimetric observations with the upgraded GMRT (uGMRT). We have carried out test observations with the Walsh modulation enabled and disabled, using the polarized calibrator 3C138 as the target and unpolarized calibrator 3C147 for amplitude, phase and leakage calibration. The two Walsh datasets (ON & OFF) were processed using identical flagging, calibration, and imaging procedures. The Walsh ON data showed smoother leakage solutions and lower residual scatter in the Stokes I, Q, U data. The Walsh scheme also suppressed a fraction of anomalous data at the correlation stage, reducing the amount of subsequent flagging required during data processing. The final self-calibrated images obtained with Walsh ON showed improved image fidelity, lower r.m.s. noise, and reduced error in the fractional polarization of 3C138. However, the possibility that the Walsh scheme itself introduces spurious frequency-dependent structure into the Stokes Q and U signals remains and warrants closer investigation. Overall, these results demonstrate the potential of Walsh phase switching to mitigate instrumental cross-talk and improve the reliability of polarimetric measurements with the uGMRT.

Abstract: We present a multi-method machine-learning study for the binary photometric classification of point-like and extended sources in the Rubin Observatory Data Preview 1 (DP1) and Data Preview 2 (DP2). The workflow runs on the Rubin Science Platform using DP1 and DP2 ugrizy photometry, the DP1 ancillary photometric-redshift catalog, and the Monster astrometric and photometric reference catalog, without relying on external spectroscopy. Six methods (SVM, Random Forest, XGBoost, k-Nearest Neighbors, an Artificial Neural Network, and a soft Voting ensemble) are benchmarked in the deepest DP1 field, the Extended Chandra Deep Field South. These methods reach 10-fold cross-validation macro-F1 scores of 0.95-0.97 on the balanced training subset and test macro-F1 scores of 0.87-0.92 on the imbalanced held-out set, with RF and the SV ensemble tied at the top (0.918); the differences among the leading methods are within the statistical uncertainty. Permutation importance identifies FWHM as the dominant feature, and the six methods agree on 79.5% of the full catalog. The same pipeline is then applied without retuning the settings to the two other DP1 fields with full ugrizy coverage and photometric-redshift support, Euclid Deep Field South and the Low Galactic Latitude Field. Together, the three fields span a factor of approximately 7 in E(B-V) and approximately 10 in stellar density, yet headline macro-F1 remains above 0.87 in every field. We then apply the same pipeline to four DP2 fields (ECDFS, COSMOS, EDFS, and ELAIS-S1). Using colors and morphology, the best method reaches a test macro-F1 of 0.83 to 0.88 across these fields, and the six methods agree on 77 to 86% of each catalog. Adding the per-band photometric uncertainties as features raises the best macro-F1 to about 0.95 in ECDFS DP1 and 0.97 in ECDFS DP2 (and to approximately 0.97-0.98 in the other fields).

Abstract: The interplay between the baryonic matter in galaxies (gas, dust, stars) has a fundamental role in our understanding of galaxy formation and evolution through cosmic time. The (sub)mm regime provides a unique window to measure the physical conditions and chemistry in the cold and dusty gas reservoirs that fuel the formation of new stars and the growth of massive black holes in distant galaxies. Large sub(mm) interferometers have made a revolutionary impact mapping the cold gas and dust in galaxies beyond the local universe. However, significant progress is hampered by limited spectral-line sensitivity. After more than a decade of surveys, molecular gas (traced by 12CO) has been measured only in a few tens of typical, massive main-sequence star-forming galaxies at z>1, while access to fainter gas-tracing species has been limited to only the very brightest galaxies. Thereby it restricts access to the detailed conditions in the cold gas clouds in typical galaxies in the distant universe. A revolutionary, flagship sub-mm interferometer with a massive increase in instantaneous spectral line sensitivity and broad spectral grasp (ALMA2040) fills this gap by enabling rich, multi-transition, multi-species and multi-isotopologue studies of the atomic and molecular interstellar medium for thousands of typical galaxies across galaxy populations and redshift. This will unveil how the ISM evolves across the galaxy population for statistical samples in different redshift bins, detailed studies of the physical conditions and chemistry in the multi-phase interstellar medium of typical galaxies, and how the cold ISM regulates and responds to star- and black-hole formation, providing a tremendous leap forward in the study of galaxy assembly over cosmic time.

Abstract: Molecular isotopic ratios provide a unique window into the chemical evolution of the interstellar medium, linking stellar nucleosynthesis and Galactic chemical evolution to the physical and chemical processes shaping molecular clouds, star-forming regions, and planetary systems. However, molecular isotopic ratios do not necessarily trace the underlying elemental composition, as chemical isotopic fractionation, isotope-selective photodissociation, grain chemistry, and radiative-transfer effects can significantly alter the observed values. Disentangling these effects requires simultaneous observations of multiple transitions, isotope systems and molecular families, including rare, doubly, and multiply substituted isotopologues involving D, $^{13}$C, $^{15}$N, $^{17,18}$O, $^{29,30}$Si, and $^{33,34,36}$S (e.g., $^{13}$C$^{15}$N, $^{13}$C$^{34}$S, and $^{13}$C$^{18}$O). ALMA2040, with an order-of-magnitude improvement in sensitivity and at least 512 GHz of instantaneous bandwidth, will make these species accessible across statistically significant samples of Galactic and nearby extragalactic sources. By enabling systematic isotopic inventories from molecular clouds and prestellar cores to protoplanetary systems, across the Milky Way and in external galaxies, ALMA2040 will establish molecular isotopes as quantitative probes of chemical inheritance, stellar nucleosynthesis, and galaxy evolution, disentangling local chemical processing from the large-scale evolution of matter from stars to new planetary systems.

Method: 在空间望远镜科学研究所的HiCAT仿真器上评估模态EKF性能。对关键变量进行参数扫描：可变形镜（DM）漂移、望远镜孔径直接光子速率（决定暗区平均光子数）、以及提供相位分集的抖动幅度。引入基于协方差的EKF框架方法，以最优确定所需探测（probe）幅度。

Abstract: Long-term wavefront stability will be critical for the upcoming Habitable Worlds Observatory which aims to directly image exo-Earths. Mechanical and thermal instabilities induce slow, high-order wavefront errors which can degrade the contrast and in turn, the scientific yield of the observatory. Dark zone maintenance (DZM) has been proposed as a high-order wavefront sensing and control strategy to maintain contrast despite wavefront error drifts. This approach uses an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) to estimate the electric field at each pixel in the dark zone of the science camera. However, relying on individual pixel information to estimate wavefront errors can discard information stored in the underlying wavefront. This is a fundamental limitation on the best achieved contrast compared to the model based theoretical bounds derived from photon noise. An alternative EKF can incorporate more holistic information by estimating the drifting wavefront modes present in the system. Abstract: Radio frequency interference (RFI) is one of the biggest challenges facing current radio astronomy experiments. Telescopes like the Canadian Hydrogen Observatory and Radio transient Detector (CHORD) need to accurately identify RFI to deliver on their science goals. For applications like HI intensity mapping for precision cosmology, long integrations consisting of hundreds to thousands of hours are needed, and thorough RFI flagging is required to avoid contaminating deep datasets. Currently, many major precision cosmology instruments are facing systematic limitations that include unflagged RFI contamination. For transients, false triggers due to RFI are common and interfere with the detection of real sources like fast radio bursts (FRBs). This paper presents a prototype design for an auxiliary array of horizon-pointed antennas that are cross-correlated in real-time at full cadence with the main telescope to assist in the identification and mitigation of RFI for the CHORD instrument. The major benefit of the version proposed here over a more traditional omnidirectional observatory monitor is the capability to synchronize with CHORD in real time and provide fine timescale flags and cross correlations. The general setup and techniques used here will be applicable to other radio telescopes as well.

Abstract: Polarization reveals details of circumstellar disks that are not readily available from total intensity observations. Via multi-wavelength polarimetric observations of disks, we can trace how planets form, how dust grains evolve at the onset of planet formation, and how magnetic fields (B-fields) influence accretion. Since polarized emission from these disks is typically at levels from 0.5% to a few percent of the total emission, very high sensitivities are needed. Current ALMA capabilities make these key measurements of protoplanetary disks challenging due to (1) long integrations with separate observations to measure multi-wavelength polarization of substructures, (2) prohibitive time requests to observe large samples of disks, (3) limited sensitivity and frequency coverage for polarization in spectral lines; and (4) insufficient calibration of polarization in all the Stokes parameters. An ALMA2040 with large collecting area, increased bandwidth with multi-band capabilities, and accurate polarization calibration will provide a great leap in our understanding of the formation of protoplanets through polarization. Specifically, such an ALMA2040 can use polarized emission from the continuum and spectral lines to: (A) provide stringent tests of streaming instabilities; (B) accurately constrain grain shape, size, porosity, and alignment; (C) resolve polarization of substructures for a large sample of disks with resolutions less than 5 au; (D) quickly survey 100s of disks; and (E) measure the B-field strength and morphology of disks.

Result: 结果：宽光谱覆盖可同时探测多条CO跃迁、[CI]、H2O、反馈示踪物、远红外精细结构线与尘埃连续谱，在同一批观测中约束分子气体、星际介质条件、被遮蔽的恒星形成及气体流动；每一次定点观测也都会变成一个分子深场，将测量扩展到偶然出现的星系。

Conclusion: 结论：结合未来的HI、光学与近红外巡天，ALMA2040将实现对宇宙时间内重子循环的群体分辨视角，并对星系形成模型进行严格检验。

Abstract: Galaxy evolution is governed by the baryon cycle: gas accreted from the cosmic web and circumgalactic medium condenses into molecular gas, forms stars, and is redistributed by stellar and AGN feedback. Tracing the molecular phase is therefore essential to understanding how galaxies acquire, consume, and eject their baryons. Yet current molecular-gas surveys remain limited by sensitivity, area, cosmic variance, and spectral bandwidth.
  ALMA2040 would combine a $\sim10\times$ sensitivity improvement with $\gtrsim512$ GHz simultaneous frequency coverage, delivering $\sim100\times$ faster mapping at fixed depth and a further $\gtrsim25\times$ gain in instantaneous bandwidth. Together, these advances would increase spectroscopic survey speed by orders of magnitude, enabling tiered ``wedding-cake'' strategies: an ultra-deep $\sim4$-5 arcmin$^2$ field probing faint and high-redshift reservoirs, a $\sim$0.25 deg$^2$ survey reaching molecular-line sensitivities comparable to current ultra-deep fields over a much larger cosmological volume, and thousands of independent pointings minimizing cosmic variance.

Every targeted observation would also become a molecular deep field, extending these measurements to serendipitous galaxies. Together with future HI, optical, and near-IR surveys, ALMA2040 would enable a population-resolved view of the baryon cycle across cosmic time and stringent tests of galaxy formation models.

Abstract: Cosmic microwave background (CMB) observations are increasingly performed by using a combination of both small aperture telescopes (SATs) and large aperture telescopes (LATs) to extract information across a wide variety of angular scales. The observation strategy of the telescopes must balance the restrictions of the observing site, instrument constraints, and a variety of science goals. We present a general framework for simulating the performance of different observation strategies. Using this framework, we focus primarily on the inflationary science goal and determine the optimal LAT coverage for delensing SAT measurements and present a novel deep scan strategy for an experiment in Chile that improves observation efficiency in the deepest 3% of the sky by 26%. Finally, we present considerations for future surveys to consider to further increase their observing efficiency.

With robust chemical inventories towards large source sample sizes, we will be able to probe for dependencies of chemical complexity on mass regime, feedback processes in a cluster, Galactic position, metallicity, and time-variable accretion. Only with statistically significant samples at high sensitivity will we be able to assess the ubiquity and homogeneity of COM chemistry across the protostellar mass spectrum and natal cores. Constraining the diversity and availability of such species is critical for our understanding of the chemical reservoir available for planet formation, which eventually shapes (exo)planetary atmosphere compositions.

TL;DR: CHARIZARD是一个面向射电干涉测量的分布式流水线,将原始测量集经标记、交叉定标、自定标和成像处理为全斯托克斯图像,通过任务重分区和调度实现高效并行,在MeerKAT数据上验证了其精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射电干涉仪带宽和灵敏度不断提升,数据量庞大,手工处理困难、自动处理缓慢;瓶颈往往不在算法本身,而在于计算任务的布局方式。因此需要一种通过工作分配来应对海量处理需求的方案。

Method: 构建CHARIZARD流水线:将观测频带划分为在交叉定标中保持独立的子频带;以目标天区与子频带对作为自定标的工作单元;对不相交数据的处理阶段并行运行。每个阶段是普通PBS或SLURM作业,由与调度器无关的层提交跟踪;所有计算运行在单一无根容器中,使无管理员权限的用户在任何集群上获得一致行为。

Result: 在MeerKAT全偏振定标数据上:轴向残余仪器偏振线性0.13%、圆偏振0.06%;最深场在787 MHz达到4.11 μJy/beam,σ_I/σ_V=1.010;位置与RACS-low吻合优于0.4″,流量密度中位比值1.06。1.5 TB数据在18.4小时内完成48.6核小时的工作(含自定标与偏振定标)。

Conclusion: 下一代射电望远镜将面临更大的数据量,CHARIZARD表明通过对工作进行合理分布,可为应对巨大处理需求提供有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Radio interferometers now deliver wide bandwidths and high sensitivity, and with them data volumes that are awkward to reduce by hand and slow to reduce automatically. The limit is not always the algorithms themselves but the way the work is laid out across the computing available. We present CHARIZARD, a pipeline that carries a raw measurement set through flagging, cross-calibration, self-calibration and imaging to full-Stokes images, and which is built around distributing that work. The observing band is repartitioned into sub-bands that stay independent through cross-calibration, the unit of work becomes the target field and sub-band pair at self-calibration, and stages touching disjoint data are run together. Each stage is an ordinary PBS or SLURM job, submitted and tracked by a scheduler-agnostic layer, and every computation runs inside a single rootless container, so a user holding no administrative rights gets the same behaviour on any cluster. We benchmark the pipeline on MeerKAT measurements with full polarization calibration. Residual on-axis instrumental polarization is $0.13$ per cent in linear and $0.06$ per cent in circular; the deepest field reaches $4.11\,μ$Jy~beam$^{-1}$ over $787$~MHz with $σ_I/σ_V = 1.010$; positions agree with RACS-low to better than $0.4\arcsec$ and flux densities to a median ratio of $1.06$. A $1.5$~TB track completes $48.6$ core-hours of work in $18.4$~hours with self-calibration and polarization calibration. The next generation of the radio telescopes will not be short of data. CHARIZARD attempts to show how the distribution of the work provides a useful alternative for handling the staggering processing requirements.

</details>
